The following user(s) said Thank You: cuimingzhao QUICK REPLY REPLY QUOTE THANK YOU About the 3Drive #228 https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/homework-3drive-to-s-d/29-about-the-3drive?start=225 3/13 7/27/2018 About the 3Drive - Page 10 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT Atari Offline RTM Moderator Posts: 172 Thank you received: 421 To me, 3-Drive is like a compression,or more so, like stack zones. 3 drives normally signifies the end is near and we are likely to get a reversal, especially if it is driving directly to the potential level. Many retailers are taught to look for this pattern at turning points. But what you should focus more on is, is the 3-drive happening (driving up) to important levels we here price is likely to reverse. At that level, then you look for PA and not blindly entering expecting a reversal, because without PA you are gambling The following user(s) said Thank You: salivan, Canadough, cuimingzhao, traderscape, nsikandar "If you focus on the one thing and stay with it long enough, you will master it!" Yesterday, as my students were discussing trades after an NLP session one of club memebers asked , Why is it Mr Gibson that its ok for you to violate rules as to size, stops, and risk management, yet we are told not to do those things? I was embarrassed with no satisfactory answer. So for the remainder of my classwork and homework time I will remember that voice as it really said ...... "If you can why can't I" Often as adults we rationalize why its ok..... and as a trader I see that is a very negative action. Discipline is strict adherence to your plan. General rule regarding RR is that your trades should be at least 3:1 RR. On this forum you could quite often see trades 1:6, 1:7 or even 1:15. If you really want to be impressed check IF's journal where you can find 1:20 or 1:50 RR trades :P They are possible. I know that even from my experience (I think about 1:7 RR trades here). From reasons I gave you above I think that you shouldn't bother you with trades with 1:1 RR even if the PA confirm the trade. The trading opportunities are countless, but you money is not :) 3:1 RR (and above) may save your account. I remember that 1,5 year ago I take one trade which was 1:10 RR and after that I have 8 losers in a row... Thanks to RR I was still earning money (I was +2 R). Torosar wrote: That is just the thing.. The true supply was within reach just above and a possibility of a fakeout is very real. If I were to enter on that 3drive my risk-reward would at best be 1:1. Many retailers are taught to look for this pattern at turning points. But what you should focus more on is, is the 3-drive happening (driving up) to important levels we here price is likely to reverse. Page 3 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT Nosbigs Visitor RR 6:1 Tight Stop, Sized appropriately. Yesterday, as my students were discussing trades after an NLP session one of club memebers asked , Why is it Mr Gibson that its ok for you to violate rules as to size, stops, and risk management, yet we are told not to do those things? I was embarrassed with no satisfactory answer. So for the remainder of my classwork and homework time I will remember that voice as it really said ...... "If you can why can't I" Often as adults we rationalize why its ok..... and as a trader I see that is a very negative action. Discipline is strict adherence to your plan. General rule regarding RR is that your trades should be at least 3:1 RR. On this forum you could quite often see trades 1:6, 1:7 or even 1:15. If you really want to be impressed check IF's journal where you can find 1:20 or 1:50 RR trades :P They are possible. I know that even from my experience (I think about 1:7 RR trades here). From reasons I gave you above I think that you shouldn't bother you with trades with 1:1 RR even if the PA confirm the trade. The trading opportunities are countless, but you money is not :) 3:1 RR (and above) may save your account. I remember that 1,5 year ago I take one trade which was 1:10 RR and after that I have 8 losers in a row... Thanks to RR I was still earning money (I was +2 R). Torosar wrote: That is just the thing..
